Let's think of a known formulation: Minimize max convolution = minimize spectral norm? The convolution C = a*a. If we want C_k ≤ M, this is equivalent to: for all k, ∑_{i+j=k} a_i a_j ≤ M. This is a quadratic knapsack. Could use bisection + SDP relaxation to get lower bound? Not needed.

So for each i, ∑_k λ_k a_{k-i} = μ. This is a linear convolution equation: a convolved with λ equals constant (where λ is dual weights on active lags). Also active constraints C_k=1. This resembles designing a nonnegative sequence whose convolution with a measure is constant. If λ is supported on active lags. Implementation plan in final code:
- Define N = len(height_sequence_1) if exists else 3809.
- Helper exact-ish score using np.convolve? For final maybe evaluate_sequence. Fast objective via FFT with N_fft.
- Use optimize_block function:
  Input base block lengths? Simpler: use M blocks equal length b = N//M, remainder. Values vector v length M. Expand to a.
  Objective can be computed on a using internal fast_score. Gradient: compute soft max or active average of a, aggregate by block.
  Use Adam with nonnegative params. But block values may need scale; we can normalize a after each update to sum=1 (or keep sum). Since objective scale invariant, we can normalize a to sum=1 for gradient but if block values are the parameters, normalizing a changes relationship. Better optimize unnormalized v and after each step project v to nonnegative, and maybe scale v? Since objective invariant, we can fix sum(v) or sum(a)=M*average? To avoid norm drift, after update set v = max(v,0), then optionally rescale v so sum(a)=1? But if v parameters, rescaling v changes sum of a. We can define optimization in v direction: a=expand(v); normalize a to sum 1 each iteration for gradient? Then the corresponding v_eff = a / block_counts? If we rescale a, we can recover v = a aggregated? For block-constant a, if we modify a and then renormalize, block-constant property might break if we apply full gradient? But we can restrict: compute gradient for a, aggregate per block, update v, then expand. The sum of a changes; we can rescale v by 1/sum(a). This is equivalent to moving on simplex of v weighted by block counts. Since objective invariant, we can normalize v so sum(a)=1 by setting v = v / avg? Actually sum(a)=∑ v_j * count_j. Let counts c_j. We can maintain ∑ v_j c_j =1. After update v_new, project nonnegative, then scale v_new = v_new / (∑ v_new c_j) to return to simplex. This is fine.
  Gradient of objective w.r.t v (on simplex) can be computed as gradient w.r.t a aggregated: since Msoft depends on a, dM/ dv_j = ∑_{i in block j} dM/da_i. Normalization constraint scale invariant, gradient on simplex is projection; simple scaled block gradient works.

  Soft max temperature: Let c = conv(a,a). Use p = exp(c / T - maxc) / sum. Need T relative to c values. Since a normalized sum=1, c values ~1/n? For n=3809, c max maybe ~0.0003? Actually uniform sum1: each a=1/n, conv center=n*(1/n^2)=1/n ≈0.000262. Differences small. T should be adaptive: T = max(c) * 0.1? Or T = std + 1e-12? If T too small, p spikes. For gradient of true max, use active set: p uniform over lags where c >= M - eps. Eps maybe 0.05*M + 1e-12. That may be stable.

def fft_convolution(a):
    n=a.size
    N=1
    while N < 2*n-1: N <<=1
    f=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(f*f, N)[:2*n-1]
    return c
```

def grad_from_p(a,p):
    n=len(a); L=2*n-1
    pr=p[::-1]
    Mfft=1
    while Mfft < 3*n-2: Mfft <<=1
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,Mfft)
    P=np.fft.rfft(pr,Mfft)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(A*P,Mfft)
    # g[i]=conv[L-1-i]
    g=conv[L-1:- (L-1)-1? need indices]
```

def expand(v):

def aggregate(grad_a):

Detailed algorithm:
1. Set n = len(height_sequence_1) if exists else 3809.
2. Define internal objective fast_score(a):
   - a = np.asarray float, clip 0, maybe cap 1000 (not needed normalized).
   - if sum <=0 return inf.
   - Use FFT convolution length N=power2 >= 2n-1.
   - c = irfft(rfft(a,N)*rfft(a,N))[:2n-1]
   - To avoid tiny negative, c=np.maximum(c,0)
   - M=c.max(), S=sum(a)
   - return 2*n*M/S^2
   Potential issue: FFT of nonnegative small values; roundoff.

3. Define gradient_active(a, T_mode):
   - c = fast_conv(a)
   - M = c.max()
   - p: use entropic weights: x=(c-M)/T; p=exp(x); p/=sum.
     T = max(0.05*M, 1e-300) maybe. But if M small, T small; p may underflow. Use stable: x = (c-M)/T, x > -50 clipped.
   - Compute g by FFT convolution with reversed p as above.
   - return 2*g.
   We can also use p from top capacity: p = np.clip((c - (M - delta))/delta,0,None); p += eps; normalize. This may be more/less peak-focused. Maybe combine: p = ((c/M)**gamma) normalized? For c/M in [0,1], gamma high e.g.10 focuses peaks. This avoids T. Let's use p = np.maximum(c/M - (1-alpha), 0, alpha)?? 
   Simpler: p = np.exp((c - M) / (0.1*M + 1e-300)); This is equivalent cap top ~10%? If c = 0.9M, exp(-1)=0.37, still large; if 0.8M exp(-2)=0.135. Too broad. Use temperature 0.01M: exp(-10)=4e-5 for 0.99M. Good. But if M very small, T tiny. Use T=max(0.01*M, 1e-300).
   Could anneal temp from 0.2 down to 0.005.

Could implement backtracking:
```
cur_score = score(a)
z = log(a+eps)
eta = 1.0
for iter:
   a = softmax(z)
   c, M, p
   H = cross(a,p)/2
   Hc = H - (a@H)
   best_eta=eta; best_sc=inf
   for trial in [eta*0.5, eta, eta*2?]:
       z_try = z - eta_trial*Hc
       a_try = softmax(z_try)
       sc=fast_score(a_try)
       if sc<best_sc...
   z = z - best_eta*Hc
   if best_sc < cur - ...: eta*=1.1 else eta*=0.8
```

Compute H via FFT:
```
def step_direction(a,p):
   n=len(a); L=2*n-1
   # choose N >= 3*n-2
   N=1
   while N < 3*n-2: N <<=1
   A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
   P=np.fft.rfft(p[::-1],N)
   conv=np.fft.irfft(A*P,N)
   H=0.5*conv[L-1:-(L-1)-1:-1]? Need slicing.
```

Let's derive block log gradient correctly.
Let block counts c_j (positive ints). Let θ_j. Let G_j = exp(θ_j + log c_j)/Z, Z=∑ exp(θ_l+log c_l). Then block height H_j = G_j / c_j. Sum a=∑ H_j c_j=∑G=1. Need smooth objective M(a). We approximate active/p soft M. Gradient w.r.t H: g_j per element; block aggregation B_j = ∑_{i∈j} g_i (g=dM/da_i). Then gradient w.r.t θ_l = ∑_j B_j * dH_j/dθ_l.
Given G softmax of u=θ+logc. dG_j/dθ_l = G_j(δ_jl - G_l). dH_j/dθ_l = G_j(δ_jl - G_l)/c_j.
So gradθ_l = ∑_j B_j G_j(δ_jl-G_l)/c_j = G_l [ (B_l G_l/c_l) - ∑_j B_j G_j/c_j ]. Since H_l=G_l/c_l. Let S = ∑_j B_j H_j = a dot g. Then gradθ_l = G_l (H_l B_l - S).
As earlier. The factor 2 if g=2H. Good.
So for block objective, we can compute g via p (dM/da=2Hconv), B, S, gradθ.
Then use L-BFGS-B with finite diff? We can pass jac.

Initial block values functions for M:

def make_block_shape(kind, M, rng):
   x=np.linspace(0,1,M,endpoint=False)
   if kind=='uniform': v=1+small noise
   'ramp': 1 + r*x
   'step': v[:M//2]=1, v[M//2:]=c
   'sigmoid': 1 + amp/(1+exp(-slope*(x-0.5)))
   'poly': 1 + amp*x**p
   'geom': r**x? etc
   # normalize to weighted sum 1
```

Run block optimization:
```
def run_block_gradient(v, counts, budget_s, name):
   z=np.log(v+1e-300); z=z-z.max()
   m=np.zeros(M); vhat=np.zeros(M); t=0; lr=0.05
   best_v=v.copy(); best_score=inf
   while time<deadline:
      G=softmax(z+logc)
      H=G/counts
      a=expand(H)
      score,c=score_conv(a)
      if score<best_score: best= a, score
      temp_frac = 0.2*max(0.05, exp(-t/500))? maybe
      p=active_weight(c,temp_frac)
      Hconv, _ = step_direction(a,p)
      B=np.zeros(M); for j: B[j]=Hconv[start:stop].sum()
      S=(Hconv*a).sum()
      Glog=softmax? G already.
      grad=G*(H*B - S)
      # optional normalize grad by maxabs? Adam.
      Adam update z
      z=np.clip(z,-60,60)
      # if no improvement 200, reduce lr
   return best_a
```
`step_direction` returns Hconv (g/2). For gradient, g=2Hconv, but factor constant; use Hconv.
Does `S` use Hconv or g? If gradθ = G(HB - S) with B sum of g? If we set B=sum Hconv, S=sum Hconv*a, factor 2 omitted consistently. Good.

Full gradient: Use formula `grad = a * (Hconv - np.sum(Hconv*a))`. Factor 2 omitted. Adam lr maybe 0.05.

Implementation of `compute_conv(a)`:
```
if a.size < 200:
   c=np.convolve(a,a,mode='full')
else:
   N=1; L=2*n-1
   while N<L: N <<=1
   fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
   c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N)[:L]
   c=np.maximum(c,0)
return c
```
`step_direction(a,p)` for short direct? For short M<200, can compute H direct O(n^2) using correlation loop? n=32, O1024 fine. For simplicity, use same FFT with N>=3n-2. For n small, FFT overhead okay but many iterations maybe okay (M=16, iterations thousands). We can implement direct for short:

L=2n-1
# H[i] = sum_{r=0}^{n-1} a[r] p[i+r]
H=np.zeros(n)
# for each r? O(n^2)
for r in range(n):
   if a[r]==0: continue
   H[:n-r] += a[r]*p[r:r+n-r]? Wait i from0 to n-1-r? p index i+r. For i=0..n-1, need i+r <=L-1 -> i <= L-1-r =2n-2-r. But when i+r < n, p[i+r]; when >=n, p[i+r] still valid up to 2n-2. ```

`active_weight(c, temp_frac)`:
```
M = c.max()
if M <= 0: p = np.zeros(L); p[n-1]=1; return
T = temp_frac*M + 1e-300
x=(c-M)/T
x=np.clip(x, -40, 0)
p=np.exp(x)
s=p.sum()
if s==0: p[n-1]=1
else p/=s
return p
```
For direct score, c float.

`run_log_gradient` details:
```
def run_log_gradient(a0, budget_s, lr=0.02, temp_start=0.25, temp_min=0.01, check_iter=1, name=''):
    a0 = np.asarray(a0,float); a0=np.clip(a0,0,None)
    S=a0.sum(); if S<=0 return None, inf
    a0=a0/S
    z=np.log(a0+1e-300); z=z-np.max(z)  # keep stable; but softmax_z will subtract max anyway
    m=np.zeros_like(z); v=np.zeros_like(z); t=0
    best_a=a0.copy(); best_score=score(a0)
    stall=0; lr_cur=lr
    deadline=time.monotonic()+budget_s
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
       a=softmax_z(z)
       c=compute_conv(a)
       sc=2*a.size*max(c.c) / (a.sum()**2)  # a.sum=1
       if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
           best_score=sc; best_a=a.copy(); stall=0
           # optional update global
       else:
           stall+=1
       if stall > 200:
           if lr_cur > 1e-4: lr_cur *=0.5; stall=0
           else: break
       temp_frac = max(temp_min, temp_start * (0.5 ** (t/300.0)))
       p=active_weight(c,temp_frac)
       H=step_direction_H(a,p) # g/2
       Hc=H - np.sum(H*a)
       grad=a*Hc
       # Adaptive normalization of grad? If maxabs grad==0 break
       # Adam
       t+=1
       m=b1*m+(1-b1)*grad
       v=b2*v+(1-b2)*grad*grad
       mhat=m/(1-b1**t); vhat=v/(1-b2**t)
       z = z - lr_cur * mhat/np.sqrt(vhat+1e-12)
       # keep z range: optional center z to prevent drift
       z = z - z.mean()  # softmax invariant? Subtracting constant preserves a, helps stability.
       z = np.clip(z, -60, 60)
       # every 50 iterations validate exact? internal already exact-ish.
    # Final evaluate best_a with compute_conv and return
    final_sc=fast_score(best_a)
    if not finite: fallback a0
    return best_a, final_sc
```
Subtracting z.mean() after update preserves a exactly (softmax invariant) but may fight Adam's offset? It's fine.
Potential issue: For long n, `grad=a*Hc` where Hc variations small; Adam step lr_cur maybe 0.02. z.mean subtraction may remove global scale; okay.

z=np.clip(z,-80,80)
z=z-z.max()
ex=np.exp(z)
a=ex/ex.sum()
# ensure finite, sum1
```
Since z centered, max ~range/2; fine.

Make starts for short M:
```
def generate_short_starts(M, rng, previous_best=None):
    starts=[]
    x=np.linspace(0,1,M,endpoint=False)
    starts.append(np.ones(M)*(1+1e-3*rng.normal(size=M)))
    for r in [1.2,1.5,2,3,5,10]: starts.append(1+r*x)

def run_block_gradient(v, n, budget_s, lr=0.1, name=''):
   v=np.asarray(v,float); M=v.size
   v=np.clip(v,0,None); S=v.sum(); if S<=0 v=np.ones(M)
   v=v/v.sum()
   # block heights should make sum1 with counts. If we parameterize G softmax, initial H=G/counts. Need initial G? If v are heights, not G. We can compute G = H*counts / sum = v*counts / (v@counts).
   counts = make_counts(M,n)
   logc=np.log(counts)
   G = v*counts; G=G/G.sum()
   z = np.log(G) - logc  # so softmax(z+logc)=G
   z = z - z.mean()
   Adam arrays M
   best_a=None,best_sc=inf
   while deadline:
      G=softmax(z+logc)
      H=G/counts
      a=expand H (use np.repeat? counts variable; could `a = np.repeat(H, counts)`)
      score,c = score_conv(a)
      if score<best_sc: update best_a, best_sc
      temp_frac anneal
      p=active_weight(c,temp_frac)
      Hconv=step_direction_H(a,p)
      B=np.zeros(M); for j: B[j]=Hconv[starts[j]:starts[j]+counts[j]].sum()
      Sdot=np.dot(Hconv, a)
      grad=G*(H*B - Sdot)
      Adam update z
      z=np.clip(z,-60,60); z-=z.mean() # But subtracting mean from z while softmax uses z+logc? Softmax of u=z+logc; shifting u changes? Softmax invariant to adding constant to u, so can shift u not z? We can after update set u=z+logc; u-=u.mean(); z=u-logc. Better.
   return best_a,best_sc
```

Helper functions:

```
import numpy as np
import time

def _next_pow_two(x):
    n=1
    while n < x: n <<= 1
    return n

def _compute_convolution(a):
    n = int(a.size)
    L = 2*n - 1
    if n == 0: return np.array([])
    if n <= 200:
        c = np.convolve(a, a, mode='full')
        return c
    N = _next_pow_two(L)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)[:L]
    # remove tiny negatives
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c
```
For gradient step direction, need N >= 3n-2. Use direct `np.convolve` for n<=200? Could use `np.convolve(a, p[::-1], mode='full')` and slice. For long, FFT.
```
def _step_H(a,p):
    n=int(a.size); L=2*n-1
    if n <= 200:
        conv = np.convolve(a, p[::-1], mode='full')
        # need indices L-1-n+1? Let's verify slice.
        # Want H[i]=conv[L-1-i], i=0..n-1.
        # indices: L-1, L-2, ..., L-n
        idx_start = L-1
        idx_stop = L-n  # exclusive? For n=4,L=7, want 6,5,4,3 => start6 stop2 exclusive? idx_stop=3? L-n=3. `conv[idx_start: idx_stop-1?`
        # Using slice: conv[L-1: L-n-1 : -1]
        H = conv[L-1: L-n-1 : -1]
        # For n=4: conv[6:2:-1]=6,5,4,3 length4.
        return H
    N = _next_pow_two(3*n-2)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    P = np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], N)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(A*P, N)
    H = conv[L-1: L-n-1 : -1]
    return H
```

```
def _active_weight(c, temp_frac):
    L = c.size
    M = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0:
        p = np.zeros(L)
        p[L//2] = 1.0
        return p
    T = max(temp_frac * M, 1e-300)
    x = (c - M) / T
    # clip to avoid under/overflow
    x = np.where(x < -40.0, -40.0, x)
    p = np.exp(x)
    s = p.sum()
    if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        p = np.zeros(L); p[L//2]=1; return p
    p /= s
    return p
```
`_score_from_conv(a,c)`
```
def _score_conv(a):
   c=_compute_convolution(a)
   if c.size==0: return np.inf,c
   S = float(a.sum())
   if S < 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(S): return np.inf,c
   M=float(c.max())
   if not np.isfinite(M) or M <0: M=0
   sc = 2.0 * a.size * M / (S*S)
   return float(sc), c
def _fast_score(a):
   sc,c=_score_conv(a); return sc
```
Normalize:
```
def _normalize(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,float); a=np.clip(a,0,None)
   S=a.sum()
   if S<=0 or not finite: return np.ones_like(a)/a.size
   return a/S
```
`_softmax(z)`:
```
def _softmax(z):
   z=np.asarray(z,float)
   z=np.clip(z,-80,80)
   z=z-z.max()
   ex=np.exp(z)
   s=ex.sum()
   if s<=0 or not finite: return np.ones(z.size)/z.size
   a=ex/s
   return a
```

```
def _make_short_starts(M, rng, limit=12):
    starts=[]
    x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,M,endpoint=False)
    starts.append(np.ones(M, dtype=float) + 1e-3*rng.normal(size=M))
    for r in (1.1,1.3,1.5,2,3,5):
        starts.append(1.0 + r*x)
    split=max(1,M//2)
    for c in (1.5,2,3):
        v=np.ones(M); v[split:]=c; starts.append(v)
        v2=v[::-1].copy(); starts.append(v2)
    for p in (0.5,1,2):
        starts.append(1.0 + 2.0*x**p)
    for base in (1.5,2.0):
        starts.append(base ** x)
    # random increasing
    for _ in range(3):
        inc=rng.exponential(scale=0.2, size=M)
        starts.append(1.0 + inc)
    # random
    for _ in range(2):
        starts.append(np.maximum(rng.normal(size=M),0.1))
    # normalize positive
    out=[]
    for v in starts[:limit]:
       v=np.clip(np.asarray(v,float),0,None)
       S=v.sum()
       if S>1e-300: out.append(v/S)
    return out
```
Maybe too many; limit 12.

Implement `_run_log_gradient`:
```
def _run_log_gradient(a0, budget_s, lr=0.02, temp_start=0.25, temp_min=0.01, max_iters=None, name=''):
    a0 = _normalize(a0)
    z = np.log(a0 + 1e-300)
    z = z - np.max(z)
    m = np.zeros_like(z); v = np.zeros_like(z); t=0
    best_a = a0.copy(); best_sc = _fast_score(a0)
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0,float(budget_s))
    stall=0; lr_cur=float(lr)
    while time.monotonic() < deadline and (max_iters is None or t < max_iters):
        a = _softmax(z)
        sc,c = _score_conv(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc=sc; best_a=a.copy(); stall=0
        else:
            stall += 1
        if stall > 300:
            if lr_cur > 1e-5:
                lr_cur *= 0.5
                stall=0
            else:
                break
        temp_frac = max(temp_min, temp_start * (0.5 ** (t/300.0)))
        p = _active_weight(c, temp_frac)
        H = _step_H(a,p)
        Hc = H - float(np.dot(H,a))
        grad = a * Hc
        t += 1
        beta1=0.9; beta2=0.999
        m = beta1*m + (1-beta1)*grad
        v = beta2*v + (1-beta2)*(grad*grad)
        mhat = m/(1-beta1**t)
        vhat = v/(1-beta2**t)
        z = z - lr_cur * mhat / np.sqrt(vhat + 1e-12)
        z = z - z.mean()
        z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
        z = np.clip(z, -60.0, 60.0)
    # final exact validate
    final_sc = _fast_score(best_a)
    if not np.isfinite(final_sc):
        best_a = a0; final_sc = _fast_score(a0)
    return best_a, float(final_sc)
```
Potential issue: `a0` short normalized; z log has large negative if zeros; start positive. If start has zeros, log(0) -> -inf; _normalize doesn't floor. Use `a0=np.maximum(a0,1e-300)` before log. For starts with zeros, add tiny.
```

```

def _run_block_gradient(v, n, budget_s, lr=0.1, temp_start=0.25,temp_min=0.01,name=''):
    v=_normalize(v)
    M=v.size
    counts=_make_counts(M,n)
    H0=v*counts? If v are block heights sum1 not weighted. We need initial G=H*counts. If v normalized sum1 but not weighted, to get H such that H@counts=1, set H = v / (v@counts) roughly. Better use user v as desired block heights; normalize weighted:
    H = np.clip(v,0,None); S=H@counts; if S>0 H=H/S else H=np.ones(M)/(M); 
    G=H*counts; G=G/G.sum()
    u=np.log(G+1e-300)
    u=u-u.max()
    m=np.zeros(M); vv=np.zeros(M); t=0; lr_cur=lr; best_a=expand(H); best_sc=fast_score(best_a)
    bp=np.r_[0, cumsum(counts)]
    while...
       G=_softmax(u)
       H=G/counts
       a=np.repeat(H, counts)
       sc,c=_score_conv(a)
       update best
       temp...
       p=...
       Hconv=_step_H(a,p)
       B=np.array([Hconv[bp[j]:bp[j+1]].sum() for j in range(M)]) # loop M small
       Sdot=float(np.dot(Hconv,a))
       grad=G*(H*B - Sdot)
       Adam update u
       u=u-u.mean()
    return best_a,best_sc
```

Local search function:
```
def _local_block_perturb(a, deadline, rng):
   best=a; best_sc=_fast_score(best)
   sigma=0.08
   n=best.size
   while time.monotonic()<deadline:
      cand=best.copy()
      if rng.random()<0.5:
         # block scale
         width=max(1,int(rng.integers(max(1,n//200), max(2,n//8))))
         start=int(rng.integers(0,n-width+1))
         cand[start:start+width] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0,sigma))
      else:
         # global small
         cand *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0,sigma*0.5,n))
      cand=np.maximum(cand,0); S=cand.sum(); if S>0 cand/=S
      sc=_fast_score(cand)
      if sc<best_sc:
         best=cand; best_sc=sc
         if rng.random()<0.2: sigma=min(0.3,sigma*1.1)
         else: sigma=max(0.01,sigma*0.95)
      else:
         if rng.random()<0.1: sigma=max(0.005,sigma*0.9)
   return best,best_sc
```
This can do many FFT; 680s enough maybe 2000 evals? Each FFT ~1ms? 680k? okay.

```
def _search_short(n, budget_s, rng):
    lengths = [3,4,5,6,7,8,9,10,12,14,16,18,20,24,28,32,40,48,64]
    lengths = [m for m in lengths if 2 <= m <= n]
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, budget_s)
    best_a = _normalize(np.ones(n))
    best_sc = _fast_score(best_a)
    best_v = None; best_M = 0
    per_start_s = 0.6 if n <= 5000 else 0.3
    for M in lengths:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
        starts = _make_short_starts(M, rng, limit=10)
        # maybe if best_v length close, prepend
        for v0 in starts:
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
            a, sc = _run_log_gradient(v0, per_start_s, lr=0.05, temp_start=0.3, temp_min=0.02)
            if a is None: continue
            a_exp = _expand_to_n(a, n)
            sc_exp = _fast_score(a_exp)
            if np.isfinite(sc_exp) and sc_exp < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc_exp
                best_a = a_exp
                best_v = a.copy()
                best_M = M
        # optional record simple starts without gradient if time? already.
    return best_a,float(best_sc),best_v,best_M
```
`_run_log_gradient` budget per start; for many starts total maybe lengths19*10*0.6=114s. Good.
But `_run_log_gradient` for M small with 0.6s may do many iterations; okay. It uses `_score_conv` direct for M≤200.

`_average_blocks(a, M)`:
```
def _average_blocks(a, M):
    counts=_make_counts(M, a.size)
    bp=np.r_[0, counts.cumsum()]
    v=np.array([a[bp[i]:bp[i+1]].mean() for i in range(M)])
    return v
```

For long full gradient, we can write a specialized combined function to reduce FFTs:
- N = next_pow_two(3n-2) (>=L).
- A = rfft(a,N)
- c = irfft(A*A,N)[:L]
- score from c
- p from c
- P = rfft(p[::-1],N)
- H = irfft(A*P,N)[slice]
This uses one rfft for a (reused), one rfft for p, two ifft. Our current score uses separate rfft length8192 (maybe less cost). But N16 rfft for a reused saves one and ensures same N. Write:
```
def _score_and_direction(a, temp_frac):
   n=a.size; L=2*n-1
   if n <= 200:
      c=np.convolve(a,a,'full')
      M=c.max(); S=a.sum(); sc=...
      p=_active_weight(c,temp_frac)
      convp=np.convolve(a,p[::-1],'full')
      H=convp[L-1: L-n-1:-1]
   else:
      N=_next_pow_two(3*n-2)
      A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
      c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:L]
      c=np.maximum(c,0)
      M=c.max(); S=a.sum(); sc=...
      p=_active_weight(c,temp_frac)
      P=np.fft.rfft(p[::-1],N)
      convp=np.fft.irfft(A*P,N)
      H=convp[L-1: L-n-1:-1]
   return sc,c,H
```
For n<=200, direct np.convolve for score and p conv; okay. For n=1 slice handle.

   k=min(5,n)
   kern=np.ones(k)/k
   smooth=np.convolve(best_a, kern, mode='same')
   smooth=np.maximum(smooth,0); smooth=_normalize(smooth)
```

    a=np.clip(a,0.0,None)

```python
"""
Search for a nonnegative coefficient sequence minimizing
2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / sum(a)**2.

The program combines:
- short-sequence global construction search (independent blocks),
- block-parameterized gradient descent on the target length,
- full log-simplex gradient descent,
- local block perturbations.

The objective is scale-invariant, so all internal searches are done on the
positive simplex (or equivalent softmax/block-normalized representations).
"""

import numpy as np
import time


def _next_pow_two(x):
    n = 1
    while n < int(x):
        n <<= 1
    return n


def _normalize(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, a.size)
    return a / s


def _softmax(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
    z = np.clip(z, -80.0, 80.0)
    z = z - float(z.max())
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(z.size, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, z.size)
    out = e / s
    return out


def _make_counts(m, n):
    m = int(m)
    n = int(n)
    if m <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.int64)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.zeros(m, dtype=np.int64)
    if m > n:
        counts = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.int64)
        counts[:n] = 1
        return counts
    base = n // m
    rem = n % m
    counts = np.full(m, base, dtype=np.int64)
    counts[:rem] += 1
    return counts


def _compute_convolution(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 1:
        return a.astype(np.float64, copy=True)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    if n <= 200:
        c = np.convolve(a, a, mode="full")
        return c.astype(np.float64, copy=True)
    N = _next_pow_two(L)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)[:L]
    return np.maximum(c, 0.0)


def _fast_score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    c = _compute_convolution(a)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return float("inf")
    m = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m < 0.0:
        m = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * a.size * m / (s * s))


def _active_weight(c, temp_frac):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    L = int(c.size)
    if L <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    M = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
        p = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        p[L // 2] = 1.0
        return p
    T = max(float(temp_frac) * M, 1e-300)
    x = (c - M) / T
    x = np.where(x < -40.0, -40.0, x)
    p = np.exp(x)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        p = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        p[L // 2] = 1.0
        return p
    p /= s
    return p


def _slice_step_direction(conv, n):
    n = int(n)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([float(conv[0]) if conv.size else 0.0], dtype=np.float64)
    return np.asarray(conv[L - 1:L - n - 1:-1], dtype=np.float64).copy()


def _score_and_direction(a, temp_frac):
    """
    Returns (score, convolution c, H) where H is the subgradient shape for
    minimizing the maximum of c up to a positive scale factor.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return float("inf"), np.array([], dtype=np.float64), np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    L = 2 * n - 1

    if n <= 200:
        c = np.convolve(a, a, mode="full").astype(np.float64, copy=False)
        s = float(a.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
            return float("inf"), c, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        M = float(c.max())
        if not np.isfinite(M) or M < 0.0:
            M = 0.0
        score = float(2.0 * n * M / (s * s))
        p = _active_weight(c, temp_frac)
        if p.size != L:
            p = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
            p[L // 2] = 1.0
        cp = np.convolve(a, p[::-1], mode="full")
        H = _slice_step_direction(cp, n)
        return score, c, H

    N = _next_pow_two(3 * n - 2)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)[:L]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return float("inf"), c, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    M = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M < 0.0:
        M = 0.0
    score = float(2.0 * n * M / (s * s))
    p = _active_weight(c, temp_frac)
    if p.size != L:
        p = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        p[L // 2] = 1.0
    P = np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], N)
    cp = np.fft.irfft(A * P, N)
    H = _slice_step_direction(cp, n)
    return score, c, H


def _expand_to_n(v, n):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    m = int(v.size)
    if m <= 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    counts = _make_counts(m, n)
    a = np.repeat(v, counts)
    return _normalize(a)


def _average_blocks(a, m):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    counts = _make_counts(m, n)
    bounds = np.r_[0, np.cumsum(counts)]
    out = np.empty(m, dtype=np.float64)
    for i in range(m):
        lo = int(bounds[i])
        hi = int(bounds[i + 1])
        out[i] = 1.0 if hi <= lo else float(a[lo:hi].mean())
    return out


def _make_short_starts(m, rng, limit=12):
    starts = []
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m, endpoint=False)

    starts.append(np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) + 1e-3 * rng.normal(size=m))

    for r in (1.1, 1.3, 1.5, 2.0, 3.0, 5.0):
        starts.append(1.0 + r * x)

    split = max(1, m // 2)
    for c in (1.5, 2.0, 3.0):
        v = np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64)
        v[split:] = c
        starts.append(v)
        starts.append(v[::-1].copy())

    for p in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
        starts.append(1.0 + 2.0 * (x ** p))

    for base in (1.5, 2.0):
        starts.append(base ** x)

    for _ in range(3):
        inc = rng.exponential(scale=0.25, size=m)
        starts.append(1.0 + inc)

    for _ in range(2):
        starts.append(np.maximum(rng.normal(size=m), 0.1))

    out = []
    for v in starts[:max(1, int(limit))]:
        v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
        v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
        v = np.maximum(v, 0.1)
        s = float(v.sum())
        if s > 1e-300:
            out.append(v / s)
    return out


def _run_log_gradient(a0, budget_s, lr=0.02, temp_start=0.25, temp_min=0.01, name=""):
    a0 = _normalize(a0)
    if budget_s <= 0.0:
        return a0.copy(), _fast_score(a0)

    n = int(a0.size)
    z = np.log(np.maximum(a0, 1e-300))
    z = z - float(z.max())
    m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t = 0
    beta1 = 0.90
    beta2 = 0.999
    lr_cur = float(lr)
    best_a = a0.copy()
    best_sc = _fast_score(a0)
    stall = 0
    deadline = time.monotonic() + float(budget_s)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        a = _softmax(z)
        temp_frac = max(temp_min, temp_start * (0.5 ** (t / 300.0)))
        score, c, H = _score_and_direction(a, temp_frac)

        if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(score)
            best_a = a.copy()
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall > 300:
                if lr_cur > 1e-5:
                    lr_cur *= 0.5
                    stall = 0
                else:
                    break

        Hc = H - float(np.dot(H, a))
        grad = a * Hc
        t += 1

        m = beta1 * m + (1.0 - beta1) * grad
        v = beta2 * v + (1.0 - beta2) * (grad * grad)
        mhat = m / (1.0 - beta1 ** t)
        vhat = v / (1.0 - beta2 ** t)
        z = z - lr_cur * mhat / np.sqrt(vhat + 1e-12)
        z = z - float(z.mean())
        z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
        z = np.clip(z, -60.0, 60.0)

    final_sc = _fast_score(best_a)
    if not np.isfinite(final_sc):
        best_a = a0.copy()
        final_sc = _fast_score(a0)
    if name:
        print(f"  log-gradient {name}: best={final_sc:.6f}")
    return best_a.copy(), float(final_sc)


def _run_block_gradient(block_values, n, budget_s, lr=0.15, temp_start=0.25, temp_min=0.01, name=""):
    block_values = np.asarray(block_values, dtype=np.float64)
    m = int(block_values.size)
    if m <= 0 or n <= 0:
        a0 = _normalize(np.ones(n))
        return a0, _fast_score(a0)
    if budget_s <= 0.0:
        a0 = _expand_to_n(block_values, n)
        return a0, _fast_score(a0)

    counts = _make_counts(m, n)
    H = np.asarray(block_values, dtype=np.float64)
    H = np.where(np.isfinite(H), H, 0.0)
    H = np.maximum(H, 0.0)
    ws = float(np.dot(H, counts))
    if not np.isfinite(ws) or ws <= 1e-300:
        H = np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(sum(counts))
    else:
        H = H / ws

    G = H * counts
    G = G / float(G.sum())
    u = np.log(np.maximum(G, 1e-300))
    u = u - float(u.max())

    mom = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    varr = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    t = 0
    beta1 = 0.90
    beta2 = 0.999
    lr_cur = float(lr)
    best_a = _expand_to_n(H, n)
    best_sc = _fast_score(best_a)
    stall = 0
    deadline = time.monotonic() + float(budget_s)

    bounds = np.r_[0, np.cumsum(counts)]

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        G = _softmax(u)
        H = G / counts.astype(np.float64)
        a = np.repeat(H, counts)

        temp_frac = max(temp_min, temp_start * (0.5 ** (t / 300.0)))
        score, c, Hconv = _score_and_direction(a, temp_frac)

        if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(score)
            best_a = a.copy()
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall > 250:
                if lr_cur > 1e-5:
                    lr_cur *= 0.5
                    stall = 0
                else:
                    break

        B = np.empty(m, dtype=np.float64)
        for i in range(m):
            lo = int(bounds[i])
            hi = int(bounds[i + 1])
            B[i] = float(Hconv[lo:hi].sum())
        sdot = float(np.dot(Hconv, a))
        grad = G * (H * B - sdot)
        t += 1

        mom = beta1 * mom + (1.0 - beta1) * grad
        varr = beta2 * varr + (1.0 - beta2) * (grad * grad)
        mhat = mom / (1.0 - beta1 ** t)
        vhat = varr / (1.0 - beta2 ** t)
        u = u - lr_cur * mhat / np.sqrt(vhat + 1e-12)
        u = u - float(u.mean())
        u = np.where(np.isfinite(u), u, 0.0)
        u = np.clip(u, -60.0, 60.0)

    final_sc = _fast_score(best_a)
    if not np.isfinite(final_sc):
        best_a = _expand_to_n(H, n)
        final_sc = _fast_score(best_a)
    if name:
        print(f"  block-gradient {name}: best={final_sc:.6f}")
    return best_a.copy(), float(final_sc)


def _search_short(n, budget_s, rng):
    lengths = [3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8, 9, 10, 12, 14, 16, 18, 20, 24, 32, 48, 64]
    lengths = [m for m in lengths if 2 <= m <= n]
    if not lengths:
        lengths = [max(2, min(8, n))]

    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(budget_s))
    best_a = _normalize(np.ones(n))
    best_sc = _fast_score(best_a)
    best_v = None
    best_m = 0

    per_start_s = 0.55 if n <= 6000 else 0.30

    for m in lengths:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break
        starts = _make_short_starts(m, rng, limit=12)
        for start in starts:
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                break
            a_short, _ = _run_log_gradient(
                start,
                min(per_start_s, max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())),
                lr=0.05,
                temp_start=0.30,
                temp_min=0.02,
                name=""
            )
            if a_short is None or a_short.size != m:
                continue
            a_long = _expand_to_n(a_short, n)
            sc = _fast_score(a_long)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = float(sc)
                best_a = a_long.copy()
                best_v = a_short.copy()
                best_m = m

    if best_v is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline:
        a_short, _ = _run_log_gradient(
            best_v,
            min(8.0, max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())),
            lr=0.02,
            temp_start=0.20,
            temp_min=0.01,
            name=""
        )
        a_long = _expand_to_n(a_short, n)
        sc = _fast_score(a_long)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(sc)
            best_a = a_long.copy()
            best_v = a_short.copy()
            best_m = a_short.size

    print(f"  short search: best={best_sc:.6f}")
    return best_a.copy(), float(best_sc), (best_v if best_v is not None else _average_blocks(best_a, min(32, n))), best_m


def _local_block_perturb(a, deadline, rng):
    best_a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    best_sc = _fast_score(best_a)
    n = int(best_a.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return best_a, float("inf")

    sigma = 0.08
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        cand = best_a.copy()
        r = float(rng.random())
        if r < 0.65:
            lo_w = max(1, n // 300)
            hi_w = max(lo_w + 1, n // 6)
            width = int(rng.integers(lo_w, hi_w))
            if width > n:
                width = n
            start = int(rng.integers(0, n - width + 1))
            cand[start:start + width] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma)))
        elif r < 0.85:
            width = max(1, int(n // 20))
            start = int(rng.integers(0, n - width + 1))
            cand[start:start + width] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, max(0.02, sigma * 1.5))))
        else:
            cand *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, max(0.01, sigma * 0.5), size=n))

        cand = np.where(np.isfinite(cand), cand, 0.0)
        cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0)
        s = float(cand.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
            continue
        cand /= s
        sc = _fast_score(cand)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(sc)
            best_a = cand
            sigma = min(0.30, sigma * 1.08)
        else:
            sigma = max(0.005, sigma * 0.96)

    final_sc = _fast_score(best_a)
    print(f"  local perturb: best={final_sc:.6f}")
    return best_a.copy(), float(final_sc)


def _scale_for_return(a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    if a.size == 0:
        return [1.0]
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        out = np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64)
        return [float(x) for x in out]
    mx = float(a.max())
    if not np.isfinite(mx) or mx <= 0.0:
        out = np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64)
        return [float(x) for x in out]
    scale = min(float(a.size), 1000.0 / mx)
    if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale <= 0.0:
        scale = 1.0
    out = a * scale
    out_sum = float(out.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(out_sum) or out_sum <= 0.0:
        out = np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if out.sum() <= 0.0:
            out = np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return [float(x) for x in out]


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    """
    Main search entry point.

    Uses the global height_sequence_1 if present to fix the sequence length.
    If unavailable, uses length 3809.
    """
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    total_budget = max(30.0, float(budget_s))
    global_deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, total_budget - 5.0)

    n_target = 3809
    try:
        base = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
        if base.ndim == 1 and base.size > 2:
            n_target = int(base.size)
    except Exception:
        n_target = 3809

    if not np.isfinite(n_target) or n_target < 3:
        n_target = 3809
    if n_target > 12000:
        n_target = 12000

    n = int(n_target)
    best_a = _normalize(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
    best_sc = _fast_score(best_a)
    print(f"initial n={n} score={best_sc:.6f}")

    # Phase 1: short-sequence construction.
    short_budget = min(240.0, 0.15 * total_budget)
    if global_deadline - time.monotonic() > 10.0:
        a, sc, short_v, short_m = _search_short(
            n,
            min(short_budget, global_deadline - time.monotonic()),
            rng
        )
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(sc)
            best_a = a
            print(f"after short search: best={best_sc:.6f}")

    if short_v is None or short_v.size <= 0:
        short_v = _average_blocks(best_a, min(32, n))
    if short_m <= 0:
        short_m = int(short_v.size)

    # Phase 2: block-parameter refinement.
    block_budget = min(320.0, 0.18 * total_budget)
    if global_deadline - time.monotonic() > 20.0:
        sizes = []
        for m in (max(1, int(short_m)), 12, 16, 24, 32, 48, 64):
            if 2 <= m <= n and m not in sizes:
                sizes.append(m)
        sizes = sizes[:5]
        if sizes:
            per = max(1.0, block_budget / max(1, len(sizes)))
            for m in sizes:
                if time.monotonic() >= global_deadline:
                    break
                if m == short_v.size:
                    v = short_v
                else:
                    v = _average_blocks(best_a, m)
                a, sc = _run_block_gradient(
                    v,
                    n,
                    min(per, global_deadline - time.monotonic()),
                    lr=0.18,
                    name=f"M{m}"
                )
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                    best_sc = float(sc)
                    best_a = a
                    print(f"after block M={m}: best={best_sc:.6f}")

    # Phase 3: full log-simplex refinement.
    full_budget = min(930.0, 0.52 * total_budget)
    if global_deadline - time.monotonic() > 30.0:
        variants = [best_a]

        if n >= 5:
            k = min(5, n)
            kern = np.ones(k, dtype=np.float64) / float(k)
            sm = np.convolve(best_a, kern, mode="same")
            sm = _normalize(sm)
            if sm.size == n:
                variants.append(sm)
            else:
                variants.pop()

        noisy = best_a * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.10, size=n))
        variants.append(_normalize(noisy))

        per = max(1.0, full_budget / max(1, len(variants)))
        for i, var in enumerate(variants):
            if time.monotonic() >= global_deadline:
                break
            a, sc = _run_log_gradient(
                var,
                min(per, global_deadline - time.monotonic()),
                lr=0.022,
                temp_start=0.20,
                temp_min=0.005,
                name=f"full{i}"
            )
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = float(sc)
                best_a = a
                print(f"after full variant {i}: best={best_sc:.6f}")

    # Phase 4: local perturbations until deadline.
    if time.monotonic() < global_deadline - 2.0:
        a, sc = _local_block_perturb(best_a, global_deadline, rng)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(sc)
            best_a = a

    # Final safety: exact direct convolution check for moderate n.
    if best_a.size <= 8000:
        try:
            c = np.convolve(best_a.astype(np.float64), best_a.astype(np.float64), mode="full")
            s = float(best_a.sum())
            if s > 0 and np.isfinite(s):
                exact_sc = float(2.0 * best_a.size * float(np.max(c)) / (s * s))
                print(f"final direct score={exact_sc:.6f}")
        except Exception:
            pass

    try:
        final_list = _scale_for_return(best_a, n)
        exact_eval = evaluate_sequence(final_list)
        if np.isfinite(exact_eval):
            print(f"final evaluate_sequence score={float(exact_eval):.6f}")
    except Exception:
        final_list = _scale_for_return(best_a, n)

    # Fallback if something degenerate happened.
    if not final_list:
        final_list = _scale_for_return(_normalize(np.ones(n)), n)

    return final_list
```
